1586:GunlukTakvimDlg.GiderSQL.Lines.Strings:" select P.ID, TARIH,ODEMEYERI=case when HESAPKODU like '100%' then 'Kasa' else 'Banka' end, "	" TUR, GRUP=case when HESAPKODU like '100%' then KUR else 'Havale' end,  GIREN, CIKAN, KUR,CARIAD=FIRMA,ACIKLAMA, HESAP= HESAPADI   "	" from PLANLANAN P left outer join REHBER R on R.ID = P.REHBERID                                   "	" where TUR =71 and TARIH between 'PTARIH 00:00' and 'PTARIH 23:59'  "	" union all                                                       "	""	" select C.ID, TARIH=VADE,ODEMEYERI='Banka',TUR, GRUP=  'Çek',  GIREN = case when TUR in (1,3) then TUTAR else 0 end,  "	" CIKAN = case when TUR in (2,4) then TUTAR else 0 end,  "	" KUR,CARIAD=R.FIRMA,ACIKLAMA=NOTLAR,HESAP=NULL   "	" from CEKSENETLER C inner join REHBER R on R.ID=C.REHBERID "	"  where TARIH between 'PTARIH 00:00' and 'PTARIH 23:59'  "	" union all  "	" select KO.ID,TARIH,ODEMEYERI='Banka',TUR=111, GRUP='Kredi',GIREN=0, CIKAN=TAKSIT,KUR,CARIAD=KREDIKODU,ACIKLAMA, "	" HESAP=BANKAKREDIHESAPADI  "	" from KREDILER K inner join PLANKREDI KO on K.ID =KO.KREDIID  "	" where TARIH between 'PTARIH 00:00' and 'PTARIH 23:59' "	" order by ODEMEYERI, GRUP, HESAP"

Ay 466.51 377.38 77.50 7.75 3.88 4,622.62                        "	"DECLARE @TAKSITSAY SMALLINT,    "	"@I SMALLINT,                   "	"@BASLANGIC SMALLDATETIME,     "	"@TAKSIT MONEY,@KDVSIZ MONEY, @ANAPARA MONEY, @BAKIYE MONEY,@KALINTI MONEY,                 "	"@KUR VARCHAR(5),  "	"@FAIZORANI FLOAT, @FAIZ FLOAT,@KDV FLOAT,"	"@YENITARIH"#9"DATETIME,"	"@ONCESONRA"#9"SMALLINT,@DONEM SMALLINT,"	"@TATILKNTRL"#9"BIT"	"   "	"SQLKOMUT                                                       "	"--SET @TAKSITSAY=12 --IntToStr(TAKSITSAYSay.Value)+'                                  "	"--SET @BASLANGIC= '2009-10-27'--''Formatdatetime('yyyy-mm-dd',DateTimePickerOdemeBasl.Date)+'''        "	"--SET @BAKIYE= 100000.0 --EditFaturaTAKSIT.Text+'   "	"--SET @KUR= 'TL'--''ComboKurFat.Text+'''              "	"--SET @FAIZORANI = 18.37 ---0.0167  ---0.20"	"--SET @KDV = 8"	"--SET @TATILKNTRL = 0"	"--SET @ONCESONRA = 1 -- -1 : onceki günlere gider, 1: sonraki günlere gider"	"--SET @DONEM = 1"	""	"                           -- i = n * ( [ (1+r)^(1/n) ] - 1 ), nominal faiz hesaplama"	"                           --  (1+i / n)^n - 1"	"                           -- PMT = L[c(1 + c)^n]/[(1 + c)^n - 1]"	"                           "	"                           SET @I=0                         "	"                           set @FAIZORANI = @FAIZORANI / 100.0"	"                           if @DONEM = 0 begin --dönembaþý formülü"	""#9#9"  set @KALINTI = 0.0 "	""#9#9"  set @TAKSIT = (@BAKIYE-@KALINTI/power((1+@FAIZORANI/12),@TAKSITSAY))"	"/((1-power((1+@FAIZORANI/12),(-(@TAKSITSAY-1))))/(@FAIZORANI/12)+1) "	"          set @TAKSITSAY = @TAKSITSAY - 1"	"          set @BAKIYE=@BAKIYE - @TAKSIT"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9"   end else"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9"   begin --dönemsonu formülü : c = (r / (1 - (1 + r) - N))P "	""#9#9#9#9#9#9"   --   =(6.5/100/12)/(1-(1+6.5/100/12)^(-30*12))*200000"	""	"                              set @FAIZORANI = @FAIZORANI  / 12.0"	"                              set @TAKSIT = @FAIZORANI / (1.0-POWER(1.0+@FAIZORANI, -1*@TAKSITSAY))*@BAKIYE"	"                              "	""#9#9#9#9#9#9"   end"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9"   "#9" "	"                           set @KDVSIZ = @TAKSIT"	"                           set @TAKSIT = @TAKSIT * (1.0+(@KDV / 100.0))"	"                          CREATE TABLE #GECICIODEME          "	"                           ( SOZID"#9"INT,                      "	"                             TARIH SMALLDATETIME,            "	"                            TAKSIT MONEY, KDVSIZ MONEY, ANAPARA MONEY, FAIZ FLOAT, BAKIYE MONEY,              "	"                            KUR VARCHAR(5),                  "	"                             ACIKLAMA VARCHAR(50)            "	"                           )                                 "	"                          WHILE @I <= @TAKSITSAY -1                                                                    "	"                         BEGIN  "	"                            --set @FAIZ = (@BAKIYE*@FAIZORANI)/(100)"	"                            set @FAIZ = (@BAKIYE*@FAIZORANI/12)                                           "	"                            set @ANAPARA = @KDVSIZ - @FAIZ                                                                         "	"                            set @BAKIYE = @BAKIYE - @ANAPARA                                                                      "	"                            select @YENITARIH = DATEADD(month, @I, @BASLANGIC)"	"                            "	"                            if @ONCESONRA<>0 "	"                            BEGIN"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"if exists(select * from REHBERINI where "	"BOLUM = 'ResmiTatiller' AND DEGER = "	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9	"CONVERT(varchar(10),@YENITARIH,103)) or DATENAME(WEEKDAY,@YENITARIH) IN ('Saturday','Sunday')"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"BEGIN"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"set @TATILKNTRL = 1"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"while @TATILKNTRL = 1"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"BEGIN"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"select @YENITARIH = "	"DATEADD(day, @ONCESONRA, @YENITARIH)  "	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"if exists(select * from "	"REHBERINI where BOLUM = 'ResmiTatiller' AND"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"   DEGER = "	"CONVERT(varchar(10),@YENITARIH,103)) or DATENAME(WEEKDAY,@YENITARIH) IN ('Saturday','Sunday')"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"set "	"@TATILKNTRL = 1"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"ELSE"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"set "	"@TATILKNTRL = 0"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"END"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"END"	""#9#9#9#9#9#9#9"END"	"                            "	"                           INSERT INTO #GECICIODEME (SOZID, TARIH, TAKSIT,KDVSIZ ,ANAPARA,FAIZ, BAKIYE, KUR, "	"ACIKLAMA )                        "	"                            VALUES (1,@YENITARIH, "	"ROUND(@TAKSIT,2),ROUND(@KDVSIZ,2),ROUND(@ANAPARA,2),ROUND(@FAIZ,2), ROUND(@BAKIYE,2), "	"@KUR, CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@I+1)+'.
